Season 1

S1 Ep 1

- Hannibal is a psychiatrist introduced in the episode 1 of Hannibal.

- Jack Crawford, an FBI agent visits Hannibal's place of work and sees his drawings, which he finds extremely impressive, showing his boarding school in Paris when he was a child. Jack says the amount of detail is impressive, he also points out that it's thanks to his talent for drawing that he was able to get into John Hopkins University
(VSI, Knowledge)

- Hannibal wrote a book on the evolutionary origins of social exclusion. Hannibal’s work was so profound that after reading it, even Jack (the director of the behavioral sciences department) was viewing himself as a layman compared to Hannibal (due to his vast knowledge and understanding of psychology and his linguistic proficiency).
(Knowledge, VCI)

- Jack is immediately impressed by Hannibal, respecting him completely after only meeting him for a few minutes. Jack considers himself inferior in Hannibal’s company. (Charisma)

- Now why did Jack come? Alana Bloom from a psychology department recommended that Jack see Hannibal for a psychological profile of a killer.

- Hannibal goes to meet Will Graham, Will graham say that his thought are not tasty and Hannibal associate that directly with the possibility of Will having an high empathy considering he seem a bit troubled, he notices that Will doesn't like eye contact, Hannibal says that what he sees must affect the rest of his mind, Hannibal perceives the emotional state of Will graham, the eyes of Will are his imagination, he is shocked  by his associations and appalled by his dreams. Hannibal induced Will's ability to be able to put himself in the killer's brain and , to be able to reconstruct the scene and play it from the killer's side. Hannibal induced this with several things: the fact that he had rapid associations of ideas, that he was building fortresses to protect himself, he was avoiding eye contact, and we can directly see that Will has emotional problems when he speaks, which is again a symptom. It was Will himself who confessed these things in conversation with Hannibal, unaware that he was trying to induce his problem. By confirming all this he was able to induce Will's ability to visualize the crime scene as it happened, the associations Will makes at the scene and then visualizes terrifies him, which is why he creates fortresses to protect himself. Hannibal also showed high Emotional perception as he was able to perceive Will's emotional state by the fact that he didn’t like eye contact, because with eyes you can see too much, eyes is what WIll need to see the crime scene, then he visualizes it and starts to be terrified. Hannibal goes on to say that Will can put himself in anyone's point of view, he can perceive anyone's emotions, perception is a tool that’s pointed on both ends…
In this conversation Will also says that he can know if someone has hepatitis or burst vein, he doesn’t like looking at people because of this, he always deduces things. Hannibal smiled at him just as someone who would relate. Now here is an example of a process to guess that someone has hepatitis : You’ve been feeling unusually tired lately, haven't you? Sometimes, chronic conditions can really drain our energy levels. Have you noticed any changes in your skin or eyes recently? It's not uncommon for people to experience discomfort in their abdomen or changes in their digestion. You've probably been stressed about your health, and it seems to be affecting your mood and outlook on life. I’m sensing that there might be something related to your liver or digestion that’s been on your mind. Maybe you've had some joint pain or other systemic issues that have been troubling you. It’s often hard to keep up with daily activities when dealing with such health challenges (Cold Reading + Observation + Psychoanalysis + inductive reasoning + insight + EQP )

- Hannibal says he doesn't have a dark side that bothers him, implying that he has almost perfect Self Awareness

- Hannibal was amazed by Will's ability to understand people and he wanted to help Will find the real killer. “I think I can help good Will see his face”. Hannibal knew that Hobbs was an egotist, that if he couldn't have his daughter and wife then no one else could, so he was able to induce before Will that he ate his victims so as never to lose his "daughter". He always did this in special places, he has a place to do it he never did it outside, which means he eats them for a reason which is that he wants to keep part of their souls inside him, this is confirmed with the fact that he always kills very similar girls. He always respected his victims because he wanted to keep a part of them inside, and all the victims were the same age and looked alike, as if he was trying to keep someone inside him forever, someone who leaves home every day, who takes the risk of never coming back... that girl. (EU, STP, Inductive Reasoning). So he was able to recreate a murder with the exact same way that the Minnesota Shrike (aka Garret Jacob Hobbs) would, but also in a different way so that Will would be able to distinguish the two. (EU, Anticipation, STP : Hannibal displayed incredible emotional understanding by first understanding Hobbs to an extensive level. In Ep2 Will described Hannibal’s knowledge of Hobbs as “intimate” with complete understanding of his patterns, motives and ways of killing, making him capable of flawlessly recreating his work (and even elevating it to another level). But he also demonstrated intimate knowledge about Will. Hannibal knew that by just looking at the crime scene, Will would immediately understand that this wasn’t the real killer because he was able to understand Will’s thought process. He knew that Will would immediately find the differences between the two killers' intentions and that would help him to better understand the reason Hobbs killed (coming to the conclusion that Hobbs has a daughter). Hannibal was able to recreate Will’s entire thought process before Will himself and based on that, recreate the crime scene). And that’s only after meeting Will for a few minutes. (Anatomical Knowledge + Surgical Precision: He was able to carefully remove the organ from the body without damaging any other organs along the way (the removal of the organ happened when the victim was still alive). (Creative Thinking: Hannibal was able to find a creative way to show Will the true motives of Hobbs without interfering and at the same time conceal the true nature of his crimes by making it look like the work of a Copycat and not the Chesapeake Ripper). Also the display of the body was highly theatrical.) As Will said “He showed me a negative so that I could see the positive”. After Will had witnessed the crime scene he would better understand the Minnesota Shrike. Will was able to observe that the first had treated the victim extremely well, because he wanted to keep a part of the victim inside him by eating her, and this killer is Hobbs. The imitator, on the other hand, has treated his victim like a sow. Hobbs had a place to do this, to kill his victims and eat them. He had no interest in open-air murders, and a deer head was stolen too. Will infers that he has a house or cabin with serf trophies on the wall. Will goes on to say that the basic killer (Garret Jacob Hobbs) has a daughter who is the same age as the victims, same hair color, same eye color, same height, same weight. She's his only daughter, and he can't bring himself to lose her. That's why he eats girls who look exactly like his daughter, so he can never " lose her ". (Deception: Misdirection- Hannibal created the different psychological profile of the Copycat to misdirect Will and the FBI from him aka the Chesapeake Ripper)

- The next morning, Hannibal arrives at Will’s house, allowing him to better understand him and come close to him. Is at this moment that Hannibal feeds Will human flesh for the first time, completely blinding him with his charisma. In their conversation Hannibal makes some more observations about the killer, further helping Will while also trying to separate Will from Jack by sharing his opinion about Jack’s thoughts on Will. “I think Jack sees you as a fragile little tea cup, the one used only for special guests”.
(Social Awareness, Charisma, Verbal Deception, Manipulation:Gaslighting)

- After analyzing the evidence they found a piece of material that led them into a construction site. So Will and Hannibal are going to that location to find clues. Hobbs (who is a worker at the construction site) has been away for several days in a row. Will and Hannibal are taking some employee files to be analyzed in the FBI headquarters. So Hannibal gives them to the woman who puts them in the car, but he drops a lot of them, forcing Will to pick them up while the woman puts them in the car. Meanwhile, Hannibal, who did this on purpose, calls Garret Jacob Hobbs. His daughter answers, which confirms to Hannibal that he does indeed have a daughter, which confirms Will's hypothesis about the killer, which reinforces the fact that he is the killer. The only sentence Hannibal will say is "they know" to say that the FBI knows he's the killer and that they're on their way. Hannibal couldn't elaborate because he didn't have time, but this sentence is more than enough. I would add that he called in a masked number and was next to 2 people, which makes this action dangerous and not really thinkable, but it was all calculated, keep in mind that Hannibal has inhuman smell which would allow him to sense someone if they come.

- This sentence will push Garret Jacob Hobbs to kill his wife, if he's arrested then no one can have her, it fits his selfish personality and he'll take his daughter hostage to protect himself from Will but will decide to stab her anyway, thinking that if he's arrested then if he can't have her then no one can, Will will shoot Hobbs and kill him.

by placing himself as a friend of Will, who's with him, even in the hospital beside Hobbs' daughter, he's trying to close the awkwardness and distance between himself and Will, while separating Will from Jack

Episode Summary - Hannibal, strategically placed element in the crime scene that would indirectly lead Will to the real killer (by perfectly understanding both Will and the killer). With all these elements Hannibal was able to give Will enough information to be able to get into the killer’s mind. Will himself said that the murder created by Hannibal was a “gift” that allowed him to solve the case. He then goes on to alert the killer, by using an untraceable phone call,  just before Will encounters him. All that, in order to push Will into killing Hobbs and observe his reaction.
(Strategy, Anticipation, EU, STP)

S1 Ep 2

- Jack and Will go to find the shed with the deer heads, so Hannibal knew that Garret Jacob Hobbs had already killed many deer and deduced that he had a shed where he stored the heads and that this was also where he killed his victims as explained before. Hannibal deliberately killed a deer in the 2nd crime to make Will understand that Hobbs had a special place where he did what he did,  because compared to the copycat who killed him in the middle of a field garret do that somewhere, this is also how Will was able to understand that it was a copycat behind these murders and this is how Hannibal understood that the copycat had a place to put his trophies.
(EU, Abductive, Strategy of Episode 1)

-Jack suspects that Abigail might have been involved with the crimes of her father. Will on the other hand doesn’t want to consider that possibility. Why? Because throughout the investigation of Hobbs’s murders, Will was continually using his personality disorder to empathize with him and better understand him. This led to Will imitating the emotional state of Hobbs along with his feelings. One of those feelings was his love for his daughter and thus Will feels a special connection with Abigail (and even a heavy sense of responsibility about her).
Hannibal of course is aware of this and he even anticipated what would happen between Will and Abigail. He was able to completely understand Will’s feelings for Abigail by perfectly
understanding Will’s condition. He even understood the fact that Will views Abigail as a
“daughter” because of inheriting Hobbs’s fatherly instincts. All that from Ep2.
(Planning, Anticipation, STP, EU)

- After they returned to the FBI Headquarters, Jack believes that Will needs psychic evaluation to return to the field (after what happened in episode 1). So he suggests Dr Lecter to be Will’s therapist (after Hannibal impressed Jack in the first episode). Actually Hannibal’s original plan was to create a situation in which Will would be forced to take extreme actions and by that, push him psychologically. Therefore Will goes to see Hannibal. At this moment Hannibal tries to empathize with Will by sharing his thoughts and feelings about Abigail’s condition (which are similar to Will’s). He says that he too feels responsible for what happened to Abigail and he acknowledges the fact that they both need to help her. Hannibal is trying to create a connection between him and Will by using Abigail as a intermediary, while at the same time, Hannibal is also trying, to yet again, antagonise Jack in Will’s mind by saying things like: “What you need is a way out of dark places, when Jack sends you there”. Hannibal is slowly trying to make Will to mistrust the FBI.
(Planning, Verbal Deception, Manipulation (Gaslighting), Empathy, EU)

-After their meeting, the FBI discovers lots of corpses in the forest. We have another crime scene, so Will arrives to inspect it. When he analyzes the crime scene he sees Hobbs instead of one of the victims (seems like Hobbs’s presence didn’t go away from Will, and Hannibal was betting on it). After the analysis of the crime scene is complete Will goes to see Hannibal again.
Will tells Hannibal that he saw Hobbs in the field
Hannibal says to Will that he may have replaced one of another killer's victims with the person he considers his victim. Hannibal has also inferred that Will now finds it difficult to imagine the pleasure of killing someone now that he has killed someone. By talking about Hobbs with Will, Hannibal tries to normalize the idea of Will talking to Hannibal about his feelings and thoughts about his bark nature. Hannibal uses his charisma to make Will trust him and feel comfortable with him. Moving on to the analysis of the crime scene, Hannibal points out one important detail which is that the hands of the victim were all out of the ground. Will then deduces that the killer was cultivating his victims. He had lined up his victims, keeping them alive by feeding them perfusions, when they were found everyone was dead except 1 who died on the way to the hospital, their bodies were covered in mushrooms as if they were being used as fertilizers for something else. Their bodies were covered in fungi, which as Hannibal points out, have a structure that mirrored that of the human brain, a huge line of connections, Hannibal and Will were able to deduce that the killer was trying to create non-physical connections like those of human brains. Hannibal understands that what the killer is looking for is a form of connection.
(Psychoanalysis, EU, Déduction, Charisma, Manipulation, Knowledge)

- Afterwards , a woman arrives at Hannibal's practice, Freddie Lounds. She says that she’s looking for a therapist, and Hannibal is a candidate. On entering his office, she's like Jack, saying that Hannibal's book on social exclusion is incredible, but Hannibal stops her immediately, and asks for her bag. Hannibal knew who she was and what she was doing there. Giving her purse we learn that she had a microphone in her bag to record their conversation, in fact Hannibal knew that she was working close to the FBI as she was seen at the crime scene and sensed that she would try to record the session of Will and Hannibal that was happening just before, as at the crime scene before she had seen Will, it was explained to her that he was like a consultant for the FBI and she wanted to know more. So Basically  Hannibal must have known of her reputation and he found suspicious that she was in his office at the same time as Will graham = he dedicated that she wanted information on him. Hannibal asked her how she could know that Will had been there before and it was simply because she had to follow Will, when the policeman he gave her information about Will told her that the people she had come with were leaving, she told him they could leave without her.
(Knowledge, Insight, Abductive Reasoning)

- Back to the crime scene, how did they die? The corpses were already immersed in a mixture of hardwood, newspaper and port excrement - the perfect mix for growing mushrooms on their bodies. They died of kidney failure.

- This leaves quite a scenario, there was starch sugar in all the catheters and he would have used a dialysis pump for injections after the blood circulation stopped. Mushrooms love sugar water and so do alcoholics; give the mushrooms sugar in your body and they'll start creating alcohol. But it's not just alcoholics who suffer from endocrine disorders, they've all died of kidney failure, so they could in fact have died of diabetic acidoketosis. But Will had an insight: in fact, they were all diabetics, so they induced diabetic commas by changing their diabetes treatments and then taking them in, giving them lots of sugar to keep them alive while the circulatory system absorbed it to feed the fungi.

- 10 diabetics disappeared a short time ago after going to get their insulin, they found a pharmacist who works in all the places where the victims disappeared and he works in a place where 2 people disappeared. They search his car and find a woman under the ground, alive.

- After this event, Freddie Lounds, who was at the scene of the crime and overheard the conversation between Will and Hannibal about herself, did an article on the FBI, saying that they hire deranged people and offer them attractive salaries to arrest criminals. This action must have been predicted by Hannibal, Freddie is very interested in the FBI and had already done an article on Hobbs' cabin, Hannibal knowing that she had followed Will from the crime scene to the law firm knew that she was interested in him and with her recording afterwards of the session between Will and Hannibal showed her that Will had a pretty deranged brain. Why would she wiretap if it wasn't for her job? She didn't know Will at the time, but she was interested in FBI stories. (foresight)

- So when freddies went to Hannibal cabinet, he instantly understood that she wasn’t there to talk with him, but about Will graham, how? Well there is a high probability that Hannibal saw the article of Freddie lounds on Hobbs cabin, , from this Hannibal understood that Freddie was interested in FBI stories and had a contact there. He therefore assumed that because she was at Hannibal's cabinet, at the same cabinet of Will, she must be here to know more about him. How did she know Will was there? She simply followed Will to Hannibal's office and made an appointment right after Will so she could tap into their conversation and gain information she could use. Hannibal saw through all this, but that didn't stop him from creating his article, which the killer saw and tried to track down.
(insight, seeing through people, abductive reasoning)

- When the killer found her, he asked her to tell him where Will Graham was. Why would he do that?

-So we're going to start from Hannibal's point of view to explain everything, Hannibal created the hypothesis with Will that the killer makes a lot of non-physical connections (Hannibal had said that mushrooms resembled the structure of the brain which has lots of connectors) and Freddie confirmed to Jack that the ideas in the killer's brain move from one brain to another, mutate, evolve, he's looking for connections. So why did he want to see Will Graham? Because he knows Will could understand him - he's the only one - and he wants to help him connect to Abigail Hobbs, Garret Jacob Hobbs' daughter, which means Freddie told the killer she was in hospital. He goes there to kidnap and bury her, doing the same process so Will can connect. (emotional understanding)

- Will arrives at the hospital and manages to stop him by shooting him. At the end of the episode, Hannibal and Will are talking, Hannibal asks him who he saw when he shot the killer and Will replies that he didn't see Hobbs, it wasn't Hobbs who haunts him but the fact that he took pleasure in killing such an evil man. Will will say that Hannibal is right, killing Hobbs was just in his point of view. So Will and Hannibal begin therapy, with Hannibal helping Will understand that he took pleasure in killing someone to SAVE hobbs' daughter abigail. Also let's just go back to the beginning, why Will had seen Hobbs on a corpse, as Will says at the beginning of the episode that Hobbs is a corpse. Will, having killed Hobbs and having taken pleasure in it, now considers him a corpse, and so visualizes him on the first screen the corpse he sees after seeing hobbs killed and finally the sentence Hannibal says to Will at the beginning "is it harder to imagine the pleasure one takes in killing after having killed someone". (emotional understanding, psychoanalysis)

- In fact, what Hannibal means is that since Will has killed, he's not haunted by the fact of having killed, but by the fact of having felt pleasure when he did it. Will, haunted by this pleasure, loses himself in his brain, he doesn't know if it's because he saved Abigail, if it's because he killed someone evil. He's just lost; at the end of the episode, he'll say he felt no pleasure at all in shooting the killer, which confirms the fact that it was harder to know how much pleasure you get from killing someone after you've killed someone. Actually Hannibal was the one who psychically drove Will into admitting his deep feelings about killing so that he would use them in the future against him. Hannibal intentionally made Will admit his thoughts and feelings through their sessions (by using his charisma and acting skills to make Will lower his guard when he was talking with Hannibal), discussing it as part of his therapy, but in reality it was all part of his manipulation.
(Manipulation, Psychic Driving, Charisma)

- Hannibal's Office Library contains at least 4000 books, displaying a vast variety of subjects. From academic journals about medicine, psychiatry and anatomy to his personal collection of books that you can find here: https://weartherude.tumblr.com/post/56716405878/hannibals-library-contains-hundreds-of-rare-and/amp
Overall Hannibal’s vast knowledge in literature is enough to explain his amazing Vocabulary and Comprehension of the Language that will lead him to become one of the most well respected scientists in his field by writing articles about psychiatry that will impress even the most famous academics (like Dr Bloom).
(Knowledge, VCI, Proficiency)

-For more information about Hannibal’s knowledge go to “Hannibal’s Mind Palac and knowledge ” section

S1 Ep 3

- The episode starts with a flashback. In that flashback we can see Hobbs teaching Abigail how to hunt. We also see that Hobbs would take Abigail to hunt with him and after the hunt she would help him with his work in the cabin. After that we see Abigail waking up from her coma in the hospital.

- Jack suspects that Abigail might have had something to do with her father’s crimes so he sent Dr Bloom to talk to her.

- Abigail wakes up from the coma and goes home with Hannibal and Will. At home, she's joined by a friend, and she and her friend run into the brother of the girl who died in the 2nd murder scene in EP 1. He accuses Abigail of being involved in her father's murders, prompting Abigail's friend to throw a rock at her head, causing her to bleed. Will and Hannibal overhear their conversations and even see the stone being thrown. Hannibal sees the situation and subtly puts leaves on the stone that hit the young man; the stone has his blood on it.

-A day later, Will, Hannibal, Abigail and the police go to inspect Abigail's father's cabin once again, only for Abigail to tell them information she knows, but something unexpected happens.

- They will find a body, Will will start to inspect it

-He says that the person who killed the 2nd girl in the episode was not Garret Jacob Hobbs but a copycat, to which Hannibal will reply "I know" at which point we can see a change in Will's face as the music intensifies. In the next moment, Will looks at Hannibal as if he has some kind of intuition, but when Hannibal sees this, he immediately replies that Garret Jacob Hobbs would have paid tribute to the body. Basically Will had a kind of Intuition that he didn't even notice himself that made him feel that Hannibal was the murderer , but Hannibal instantly sensed this and tried to deflect this Intuition that Will either completely forgot about or as I said he wasn't even really aware of this "Intuition/insight".

- It’s obviously Hannibal who killed the girl, his tactic here is to use the fact that this same girl left a stone on the person who will be accused of being the prime suspect, the same person who had accused Abigail of being complicit in her father's murder

- A case uncovered like this is bound to cause a stir, so Hannibal has anticipated that when the guy walks by Abigail's house and sees all this, he'll blame himself and realize that he's a prime suspect. He'll try to sneak back into the house to tell Abigail he didn't do it, because his current state of mind is "I told Abigail she was in cahoots with her father, which is a huge insult, and her best friend threw a rock at me for saying it. She was found dead the next day, so I, who'd just lost my sister to Abigail and her father (not true, but he thinks that) is the main suspect because, as Abigail does, I'd like to take people she cares about and get revenge, but obviously since it wasn't me who did it I have to tell her, she has to believe me, because I don't want her to think I killed her friends, I don't want her to think that I'm the murderer, even if everything points against me" all this is confirmed by the fact that, before the next scene I'm going to explain, Hannibal asks Freddie, who is still at the crime scene and hanging around the house, if she has seen a man with the same physical characteristics as the main suspect.

- Now the interaction between Abigail and Nicholas, basically he just asks her to listen that he won't do anything to her like Hannibal thought, he says that because he thinks she thinks he's the murderer. But Abigail won't listen to him and will even get very scared, which will push him to catch her because he absolutely wants to tell her the truth but Abigail had a knife and stabbed him, Hannibal at the same time knocked out Alana Bloom for 1 second so that she wouldn't see the blood on Abigail's hands.

- Now Hannibal must have anticipated this scenario, he's the kind of person who never leaves anything to chance, absolutely nothing, but how?

- Abigail had probably brought her friend's bag into the house with her, and when she saw a new body, especially that of her best friend, Abigail was shocked,  an emotional shock that made her think back to her father who said "no part of her will be lost" because he ate his victims, after seeing a scene of murder of a girl it's not surprising to think back to what her father did and tell herself that the body of her friend will be lost. The bag probably belonged to her friend, or she wouldn't have held it so tightly, but the fact that it was closed made her open it, thinking back to her father's famous phrase, and as luck would have it, there was a knife on the table. As mentioned above, the guy then attacked Abigail and she killed him with the knife, so Hannibal planned and anticipated the whole scenario based on his EQU and logical reasoning.

- After that, Hannibal offers Abigail the choice of telling the truth or hiding the corpse, but tells her very clearly that, given the state of the guy's body, if she tells the truth everyone will think she was complicit in her father's crimes, which will prompt her to hide the body with Hannibal, who would be willing to jeopardize her career.

- After discreetly disposing of the body, while the police were themselves extremely busy with the murders and people trying to get into the crime scene, we move on to Alana Bloom explaining that she doesn't remember a thing, Hannibal knocked her out without even showing his face and in 1 second, Jack Crawford explains that Nicholas, the first suspect and the person Abigail killed, first hit Abigail, then knocked out Alana Bloom and then knocked out Hannibal, while his blood was found on Abigail's clothes and he matches the blood found on the murder scene.

- As said at the beginning, Hannibal subtly hid the stone with his blood, that same day he killed Abigail's best friend and put her hair in his bag, he added the guy's blood which with the scenario created just before, misdirect everyone thinking he's the suspect when it's Hannibal. What's more, I'd like to add that Hannibal was originally outside, then came into the house, but looked to see if anyone was inside, as if he knew what was going to happen.

Summary, Hannibal sees blood on the  stone ➜ hides it ➜ kills abigail's friend ➜ puts his hair in a bag + adds the guy's blood ➜ puts the body in Garret Jacob Hobbs' shed ➜ it's found ➜ a lot of noise ➜ the guy comes quietly and understands the situation ➜ wants to see abigail so that she understand that he didn't do anything ➜ Hannibal asks Freddie if she saw a guy who was therefore Nicholas ➜ meanwhile Abigail all alone has time to think about all the information accumulated ➜ thinks about her father and the bag, her friend's body must not be lost ➜ opens it with a knife on the table ➜ the guy arrives just a little later ➜ Abigail's scared since she hates him and now even more so since he killed her friend (probably) ➜ wanting to absolutely tell her the truth, he attacks her ➜ abigail kills him with the knife ➜ Hannibal knocks out alana Bloom ➜ Hannibal hides the body ➜ Hannibal misdirects everyone

- with the events, we see Abigail and Hannibal again at the end, she accuses him of being the person who phoned her father, Hannibal has literally no reaction of surprise, his expression doesn't change a bit which shows his high composure. Hannibal reveals that it's really him, and that he'll hide his secret just as she'll hide hers. With the previous and current events, Hannibal gradually begins to emotionally manipulate Abigail, who feels obliged to keep her secret because she thinks Hannibal wants to protect her.

- we also have a revelation about episode 1, Hannibal when he phoned Garret Jacob Hobbs he wanted him to be present to interrogate Will. When he says interrogate, he's talking about the hallucination Will will have after killing Garret Jacob Hobbs, in which Garret Jacob Hobbs will say "you see... you see" which you see refers to the fact that Will took pleasure in killing Garret Jacob Hobbs, so how could Hannibal have come to think that? The most logical hypothesis is that since he had induced Will's ability to be inside the killer's head, he thought that when Will killed Garret Jacob Hobbs he would feel a "pleasure" which is difficult to explain able but we can make 2 theories, Hannibal is like Will in many ways, Hannibal kills people but they're always bad people, as he says he's not a cannibal since he doesn't kill people of his "rank" they're inferior to him, he takes pleasure in it. Will being like him, in a certain aspect, would have had the same emotion. The 2nd hypothesis would be that Will, being able to put himself in the mind of a killer, would have put himself in the shoes of a killer, Garret Jacob Hobbs, and would have felt the same pleasure as Garret Jacob Hobbs did. These are the only hypotheses we can make at present, but there will probably be more information in the future.

So we know that when Hannibal called Garret Jacob Hobbs he already had the idea of having Will questioned about whether or not he felt pleasure when he was killing Garret, but it doesn't stop there. When Abigail picked up the phone and spoke to Hannibal, Hannibal was able to confirm as Will had said that Garret has a daughter and that she's in the house, Hannibal had already inferred as explained earlier that Garret wants to keep his daughter just for himself, which means that if Hannibal tells Garret that the FBI knows about the murders he'll kill her because if he can't have her then no one else will and that's what's going to happen, but why am I saying this? Since the first episodes the question that haunts Will is "did I feel pleasure when I killed" and he can't really answer, because when he shot someone else in ep 2 he didn't feel pleasure but he didn't save anyone. The fact that Will saved someone (Abigail) could be the trigger and that's why Hannibal wanted Garret to kill Abigail, so that Will wouldn't really know whether or not it was because he saved someone.
(Indirect & psychological manipulation)

S1 Ep 4

- We begin with a conversation between Hannibal and Will that confirms a hypothesis from above, Will confesses that he is extremely close to Hobbs after his death, so close that he feels they are doing the things of the day. Of course this was all Hannibal’s original plan for sending Will to Hobbs’s house expecting that this would happen. This confirms the scenario that Will when he killed Garret Jacob Hobbs was like him due to his ability and that he therefore felt this pleasure but there remains a problem, why didn't he feel pleasure when he shot the other person in episode 2? This could be explained by the fact that, as I've already explained, after killing it's harder to imagine the pleasure of killing, and Will, who doesn't want to believe that he's really had pleasure, tries to hide this reality from himself, but ends up accepting it at the end of episode 2. We could also say that, since he's already killed, what's the point of killing a 2nd time? We can also add the fact that the killer, Garret Jacob Hobbs, took real pleasure in killing, eating his victims to honor their bodies, whereas the 2nd... it was never said that he took pleasure or honored his victim, which confirms the hypothesis that Will unconsciously used his ability at that moment and felt the pleasure that Garret Jacob Hobbs would have felt. This remains rather vague, but these are the hypotheses that can be created with current information, Hannibal scales to this feat as he literally says that he wanted Garret Jacob Hobbs to question Will. Furthermore , Hannibal is creating those conversations for Will’s feelings about killing in order to record him and use them against him in the future.
(Anticipation, Planning, Strategy, Setting Traps, EU, Psychoanalysis)

-Afterwards, we can see Hannibal visiting Will’s house when Will is on a case. Hannibal has food for the dogs with him so that Will would think that Hannibal just came to feed the dogs while he was away. But in reality Hannibal was searching Will's home to find the perfect place to plant the physical evidence of all the previous murders that he had committed. He was able to find the most insignificant of places that no one would look at, which was Will’s fishing baits.
(Strategy, Tactics, Methods)

- Hannibal will take Abigail out of the hospital and into his home, sedating her with his permission. Hannibal's goal here is to get Bloom to come to Hannibal's house because she's taken his patient from the hospital without his consent, to get him to the table at 3, and since Abigail will be a little "drugged" by Hannibal's emotional manipulation of her, little by little she'll see her father and mother in Hannibal and Alana. Hannibal was able to understand Abigail's strong emotional connection to her parents and her need to feel that she belonged somewhere (the feeling of affection that her family was giving her) and wanted to simulate it on himself to create a stronger connection between himself and Abigail, so that he could further manipulate her in the future. He also used psychedelic drugs and Dr Bloom to re-enforce the efficiency of his strategy. This was part of Hannibal’s strategy to manipulate Abigail (along with his Ep3 strategy) and later use her against Will.
(Emotional Manipulation, EU, Strategy)

- Hannibal had been very protective of her during the day, saying that everything would be fine when she took the sedative, that it would help her get over the trauma, that she wouldn't have any more bad dreams. He also prepared her last meal, which she shared with her parents to touch her psychologically even more; Hannibal knew this because he either saw the meal when he was in the Ep 1 house or smelled the odors with his inhuman sense of smell. Hannibal increasingly represents a father figure to Abigail, helping her through her unhappiness, while Bloom represents a mother figure doing exactly the same thing, she's his psychologist helping him get over the disaster in his life. This is how Hannibal, by drugging Abigail a little to take her back to her memories, to see her family. By triggering her memories Hannibal is able to reenact the same feelings that Abigail felt when she was home with her old family.
(Psychological + Emotional Manipulation, Psychological Knowledge, Botany)

Summary- Hannibal (after completely understanding Abigail’s psychological state) is trying to create a strong connection between himself and Abigail by targeting her feelings about her family and making her feel the same way about Hannibal. He wants to create this connection to further expand his manipulation and letter on her connection to Will Graham that will eventually lead to his framing. At the same time he’s preparing recordings of his conversations with Will by making him admit his true feelings about killing and about G J Hobbs, establishing their connection (he’s trying to connect Will with Hobbs and Abigail with Will). Is also in this episode that Hannibal starts to plant evidence of the previous murders into Will Graham’s house.
(EU, Manipulation, Strategy, Planning, Anticipation)

S1 Ep 5

-The episode starts with Will sleepwalking in the night (which is yet another symptom of his encephalitis). Cops find him walking alone and get him home. The next morning Will visits Hannibal (his psychiatrist) and tells him about the incident. Will these days has a strong feeling of loss of control (because of his deteriorating mental state, which Hannibal created by putting Will in stressful situations like the Hobbs incident). Hannibal explains that in many cases sleepwalking can be a symptom of Post Traumatic Stress that Will feels when Jack sends him on a case. He says that Will’s experiences might have overwhelmed ordinary functions that give him a sense of control. By saying those things, Hannibal tries to create the idea in Will’s mind that Jack “manipulated” him in order to get him on the file again, which makes Will unconsciously feel resentment towards Jack along with a feeling of losing control that makes a person very vulnerable. When a person is experiencing loss of control in his life he’s more likely to take irrational action in an attempt to take back control. Hannibal takes that feeling that Will is experiencing and connects it to Jack (indirectly creating a connection between Jack and the feeling of losing control in Will’s mind).
Furthermore, Hannibal goes on to say that Will entered on a bargain with the Devil when he “decided” to follow Jack to the field (giving Will a sense of control that he needs over the situation and a feeling that he can change the situation if he wished to, which makes his manipulation more effective). Overall, Hannibal is successfully creating negative feelings between Will and Jack by psychically driving Will against Jack. (Psychological Knowledge, Psychic Driving, Direct Manipulation: Gaslighting)

-Hannibal holds an incredible sense of smell that allows him to perfectly smell and identify a woman’s perfume. When he was young he was even able to detect his teacher’s stomach cancer with his sense of smell even before his teacher knew he had one. The same way he was able to detect Bella’s lung cancer and get her as one of his patients.
(Perception)

-After finding out that the killer Will is looking for has a tumor in his brain, Will once again visits Hannibal for advice. Will states that the killer is scared because of his condition and that he feels abandoned. Hannibal immediately tries to compare Will’s situation with the killer’s, asking Will if has ever felt abandoned by Jack. While Will doesn’t feel like Jack has abandoned him, Hannibal states that Jack might have abandoned him in a way God abandons his creations (trying to make Will empathize with the killer and by that make Will feel how the killer might feel, elevating his negative assumptions for Jack). At the end Hannibal is able to help Will better understand the killer by deducting that the killer might be self destructive (even though the killer is very careful and methodical). Hannibal insists that the killer is careful of being self destructive and advises Will to scare the killer into revealing himself.
(Psychological Knowledge, Deduction, Manipulation, Persuasion)

-Hannibal once again is trying to make Will empathize with the killer, pointing out that they are both in a self destructive pattern, the only difference is that Will doesn’t have to stay in this pattern (encouraging Will to break the pattern with Jack). Afterwards, when Hannibal comes closer to Will he detects a weird smell. After smelling Will from a short distance he suspects that Will might have encephalitis that would explain most of his symptoms. We later learned that Hannibal not only could detect the condition of Will but he could also describe how exactly an autoimmune encephalitis would smell like with extreme detail (which means that he knew for a fact that Will had encephalitis). He then asks him if his headache has gotten worse and more frequent lately. By asking this question we can assume that Hannibal recognised Will's neurological problem but he didn’t say anything to Will because he wanted to see what would happen if he let Will’s condition go on.
(Perception, Information Control, Concealment, Deduction)

- Hannibal in 2 meals understood all of Jack’s couples problems and he started in this episode trying to solve them by doing sessions with that woman and Jack
(EU, Strategy)

Episode Summary- Hannibal was trying to use Will’s feelings of losing control because of his condition to separate him from Jack and the FBI by 1) making Will think that he was manipulated into returning to the field by Jack 2) making Will associate the feelings of abandonment with Jack and the FBI 3) make Will empathize with the killer in order to worsen his negative feelings (using Will’s personality disorder and empathy against him) and 4) by giving Will a sense of control over the situation and driving him to change it in order to save himself. Hannibal essentially created the idea of hopelessness in Will’s mind only for him to give him hope in the solution of breaking his pattern with Jack. At the end of the episode Will is indeed very angry with Jack and the two clash. Hannibal’s plan was successful.

S1 Ep 6

-In a prison for the mentally unstable, Gideon, suspected of being the Chesapeake ripper, goes to kill someone. He manages to kill the person after he's taken to the infirmary unconscious. Security measures had been reduced because Gideon had been cooperative for more than 2 years; he had still been handcuffed, but he had hidden a fork to free himself. Dr Chilton, the chief administrator of the hospital, claims that Gideon is the Chesapeake Ripper that the FBI has been searching all these years. Dr Chilton was also a consultant for the case the last time the Ripper had struck, which was around 2 years ago (the same time Gideon was first admitted into the hospital). That creates the possibility that Gideon might in fact be the Chesapeake Ripper. Also the crime scene matches the Ripper because it happened in a similar way as the other Ripper murders (mutilation, display of the body, surgical trophy and other factors).

-On the other hand, there are still reasons to believe that Gideon is not the ChR because of his  Impulsive behavior in the past (the ChR is always very methodical). So Will decides to conduct a joint interview with Bloom to see if he’s indeed the ChR. Gideon is trying to convince them that he is the ChR, which puts Will in some thoughts, deducing that someone (referring to Chilton) is trying to convince them that Gideon is the ChR. We also learn at the beginning of the episode that for several years the only thing the FBI has known about the Chesapeake Ripper is that he is a sociopath, and this is still only a hypothesis.

-In the night, Jack visits Hannibal’s office to talk about his wife’s condition. Is at this moment that Jack also talks to Hannibal about his former trainee, Miriam Lass.
(EU)

-During the night, Jack receives an untraceable call. In the call there is the voice of Miriam Lass, someone he hasn't seen for 2 years because she's disappeared. In the call, she asks for his help, so Jack thinks the Chesapeake Ripper made a recording 2 years ago, before he killed her, but the most important thing is that the call isn't coming from Gideon's location, which means he's probably not the Chesapeake Ripper. The real Ripper is trying to make sure that everyone knows Gideon is not the real Ripper.
(Creative Thinking, Strategy)

-After the phone call, Jack is very frustrated, so he decided to create a trap for the Ripper based on the only psychological profiles they have created for him (which is nothing more than the fact that he’s a true sociopath). So Jack believes that if they make Gideon the real ChR (by using Freddie Lounds) they’ll enrage the real Ripper and push him into revealing himself in some way. Will doesn’t think this is a good idea because by that way they might encourage the Ripper to kill again, but Jack thinks it’s worth the risk.

-Freddie makes the article regarding the ChR. The ChR is stated to be a killer who has literally been playing with the FBI for years and baffling the FBI's best profiteers for years. Freddie Lounds will do an article on Gideon saying he is most likely the Chesapeake Ripper saying he has been inactive for over 2 years as he has been in the hospital for over 2 years. Hannibal reads the article himself and sees his fame being taken away from him. Now what would Hannibal do for this?

-While Jack is interviewing Gideon in the hospital, telling him that he knows he’s not the Ripper, he suddenly gets a call from his home. Presuming that it’s his wife calling, he answers the call only to hear Miriam’s voice again. Jack, shocked by the call, immediately arrives at his house along with Will and some FBI agents. Here they find new fingerprints on the phone and hair on the bed, meaning something must have laid there.
Those fingerprints and hair samples are confirmed to be Miriam Lass’s. This scene creates the idea that Miriam is somehow still alive. The Ripper plants those elements without leaving even a shred of evidence.
(Strategy, Tactics)

- Hannibal, Chilton (head of the hospital) and Alana Bloom have a meal at Dr Lecter’s house, in which they discuss Gideon. Bloom believes that Gideon might have been psychically driven into thinking that he’s the ChR, possibly by someone like Dr Chilton. Chilton disagrees with Dr Bloom’s hypothesis, saying that it would be unethical for a doctor to use such methods, but Hannibal states that in some cases psychic drive might be useful, for example in reminding the killer about his memories if he ever suppressed them (making Chilton feel more comfortable and safe with his actions). Then Hannibal appeals to Chilton’s ego saying that “it seems that Gideon has come to that conclusion all by herself” not giving credit to Chilton and encouraging him to admit to his actions. After that, Hannibal proposes to Chilton that the two of them go and prepare the desert. Hannibal uses his charisma to make Chilton feel comfortable around him, saying that if he was in his position he would have attempted psychic driving and that he is much more forgiving of the unorthodox than Dr Bloom (empathizing with Chilton and lowering his guard). At the same time he reveals that Chilton might in fact be the one who manipulated Gideon (showing that Hannibal already knows but he wouldn’t say anything so no point in hiding it) but Chilton doesn’t answer. (Manipulation, Verbal Skills, Persuasion, Charisma)

- Miriam lass's last call can be traced, he had just called from a disposable phone. They go to the scene to find her ripped arm, with the phone in hand and a letter with the phrase “What do you see?”. Making Jack believe once and for all that it was all a false hope orchestrated by the Chesapeake Ripper.
(Planning, Psychological Warfare)

- In fact, one day during Miriam’s investigation 2 years ago, she came to Hannibal's house to talk to him about an old patient about whom she'd been having an affair. Hannibal says that unfortunately he doesn't remember much of the information she wants, which must be wrong as his memory is close to perfect, but he will say that at the time he kept a diary in which he recorded everything he did.  While Hannibal goes upstairs to fetch him, Miriam looks over her drawings and the plans for the chesapeake Ripper murders, but when she realizes Hannibal is coming up behind her and knocks her out, Hannibal had actually planned this event, since he was the killer Miriam was looking for, and he knew of this patient's involvement in the thing Miriam was investigating, he was able to anticipate the arrival of an agent in Miriam's future, and he got off the floor extremely quickly, as if in truth he'd never been looking for anything at all. (Foresight, Deception)

Episodes Summary- Miriam was missing for 2 years, presumably she was killed by the Ripper. When Hannibal talked with Jack about his trainee he was able to detect Jack’s feelings of remorse and guilt and later target them by implementing his methods. Hannibal was able to get into Jack’s head by first creating the idea that Miriam might still be alive. He did that by 1) sending Jack prerecorded messages of Miriam’s voice, calling him for help 2) breaking into Jack’s house and planting physical evidence of Miriam, like hairs and fingerprints. By doing this he was able to clear the fact that he was the real ChR and that Gideon was just a poor  copycat, without falling into the trap of Jack. With all that Jack unconsciously felt the need to help Miriam which led him to take irrational actions. After he created hope in the mind of Jack he crushed his hope by indirectly leading him into a location where he had planted Miriam’s ripped arm (making Jack angry and more obsessed with the case). Jack himself admitted that the Ripper was able to get to his head. The reason he did that was not only to disturb Jack’s thought process and mess with his feelings but also to push Jack into starting using Will more frequently in his cases (further worsening his condition via stress) because it would be Jack’s best shot at catching the Ripper.
(EU, Anticipation, Strategy, Methods, Psychological Warfare, Trap Evasion)

S1 Ep 7

-This episode starts by giving us some more insight about the Chesapeake Ripper. The Ripper kills his victims in sounders of three, he killed his first 3 victims in 9 days. Then he didn’t kill again for 18 months and then he killed another sounder of three in 9 days. The word “sounder” refers to a small group of pigs, and that’s how the Ripper sees his victims, not as people, but as pigs. 11 months after the 6th victim, the Ripper killed his 7th victim. 2 days later, his 8th victim was found dead in his workshop. The Ripper used every tool in the workshop to piece the victim and as with other victims organs were removed. The mutilation and removal of organs means someone with surgical or anatomical knowledge. Believing that, an FBI agent, Miriam Ladd was investigating all the known medical records in the area until she disappeared, she’s believed to be the Ripper’s 9th victim but no traces of her were found, until 2 years after her disappearance (referring to episode 6). Throughout the year, the Chesapeake Ripper has remained entirely and consistently theatrical. (Creative Thinking, Methods)

- Hannibal at the opera had probably entered to replay and recreate the melody of the singer's voice in his head, presumably because we can see the sound of the voice coming directly into his ear, then he closes his eyes and starts to move his head as if hearing the melody in his head, in his memory palace. He also apparently noticed the presence of one of his patients who was behind him, but there's no way of knowing how, maybe he'd just seen him before or had anticipated it because the patient himself had said he'd come thinking Hannibal would be there.
(Musical Intelligence, Awareness)

-Hannibal can easily engage in social interactions with anyone. His verbal skills and broad knowledge makes it easy for him to open conversations about any kind of subject. Hannibal used to host large dinner parties in which many high class people would come to taste his food and converse with him. The people were so impressed by Hannibal’s work that they considered it an act of performance. A lady in the opera asks Hannibal when he’s going to cook for them again and Hannibal says that he will cook when inspiration comes. The conversation is interrupted after one of Hannibal’s patients approaches Hannibal along with his friend, Tobias. Hannibal knows that this patient is trying to get his attention but he doesn’t express it at the moment, instead he greets them and starts to talk about the performance.
(Social Awareness, Social Intuition, Charisma, Cooking Skills, Knowledge)

-There's a new murderer that looks like the Ripper. Jack is very frightened to the point that he wants Will’s full attention on this one. Will says that that’s all the Ripper’s plan, that’s why he showed him Miriam’s arm, to poke him and stop him from thinking straight. Will also believes that if the Ripper was standing killing again he would be clear about it. After Will analyzes the crime scene, he concludes that this is not the ChR (despite the overwhelming similarities between the killer and the Ripper - 22 to be exact). Will suspects it's a medical student who wants to make ends meet by performing operations, only to fail.
After Will’s evaluation, we can see once again the results of Hannibal’s psychological warfare via Jack’s emotional state. Jack is now willing to break the law in order to punish the Ripper for what he has done, saying that “When I catch the Ripper you won’t get a chance to shoot him, because I’m gonna do that”, showing us that Jack has now lost his perspective because of Hannibal. Is at this moment that Jack asks Will “how do you see the killer?” meaning the Ripper (Hannibal). And even Will, with his amazing ability to see through the mind of criminals and psychopaths like it’s nothing, admits that the Ripper looks “completely normal” and that “nobody can tell what he is”.
(Deception, Psychological Warfare(E6), Planning)

-Later on the episode, we meet Hannibal’s psychiatrist, Dr Bedelia Du Maurier. She had many conversations with Hannibal over the years, but still she believes that she doesn’t know Hannibal’s true self and that she only has conversations with a version of him. She goes on to say that Hannibal isn’t completely honest with her in their conversation and that he’s wearing a “very well tailored person suit” (referring to Hannibal concealing his true nature).
(Deception, Acting Skills, Concealment, Charisma)

-After Hannibal returns to his office, he has an appointment with Will Graham. In their conversation Hannibal intentionally tells Will about the recently revealed “Ripper victim”, pushing Will to state that this isn’t the Ripper. Hannibal then tells Will that “maybe it has never been the Ripper”, making Will think about other possibilities outside of the box. Firstly, Will thinks about the possibility of being more than one Ripper, with Hannibal asking if there are any versions in the murders that might suggest that. Will says that there are. Hannibal goes on to ask Will about what was the brutalisation hiding, in an attempt to make Will think about the killer's real motive. Will states that the brutalisation was hiding the careful and surgical removal and prevention of organs, “valuable organs” Hannibal adds. Creating the idea in Will’s mind that the killer might actually be just organ harvesters disguised as the Ripper to avoid detection.
(Deception: Misdirection)

-Hannibal decides to throw a dinner party, so he goes out to gather ingredients for his menu. Hannibal kills again, this time a medical doctor that Hannibal had visited. This Doctor was extremely rude to Hannibal, so in Hannibal’s eyes he deserved to be eaten. We learn that Hannibal collects names (in the form of business cards) of people that were rude to him along with different recipes for dishes in order to eat them. The next day the FBI found the body of the doctor. After completing the similarities with the previous victim they are starting to believe that the Ripper might indeed be just organ traffickers discussing themselves as the Ripper.
(Misdirection)

-As Hannibal is preparing the dinner party along with Dr Bloom, it is at this moment that Hannibal first starts to manipulate Bloom. He starts by giving her a special drink that he himself has stored for 2 years, just for her (making her feel special). After they talk about their past, Hannibal states that the PhD candidates that Alana was looking for thought that Hannibal and Bloom had an affair in which he goes on to say “why didn’t we have an affair?".  Later Alana said to Hannibal that he and Will Graham have a common pathology, (which is to flirtatiously change the subject) Hannibal said “Or perhaps we have you in common”. Appealing to Bloom’s emotions.
(Manipulation: Love Booming)

-Afterwards, we see Hannibal preparing the food for his guests. He cooks livers, stomachs, lungs and hearts to create extraordinary dishes. Meanwhile, in the FBI Headquarters we see that 3 more bodies were discovered, all missing crucial organs. Will believes that there are 2 killers and that at least one of them is the Ripper (meaning that he can’t fully understand which of them are killed by the Ripper and which by the organ harvester). (Concealment, Misdirection, Invisibility)

-Hannibal finds Will sitting alone in his classroom. He sees all the photos of the different crimes in Will’s desk. Will asks Hannibal what he sees, and Hannibal says that the Ripper took their organs as a reward for their cruelty. Will believes that the Ripper doesn’t have a problem with cruelty and that the removal of organs happened to indignify them. Hannibal states that the Ripper took their organs because in his mind they don’t deserve them, and Will agrees. (EM, Composure, Verbal Deception)

-Meanwhile, Jack is looking at some footage from a camera. They notice a private ambulance, an ambulance with a paramedic leaving in the middle of an operation. An ambulance is the perfect place for this kind of operation, and if the police arrive, all they have to do is blend in and disappear.  They locate the ambulance and find a guy performing an operation to remove a kidney. Hannibal goes on to save this person, and we also learn that Hannibal gave up surgery one day because he failed to save a patient, but there are no further details, so we can't draw any conclusions. (Medical and Surgical Knowledge)

Episode summary- Hannibal was killing rude people for years and harvesting their organs to eat them or feed them to his guests. So in order for Hannibal to misdirect the FBI in the future he was intentionally removing not only the organs but also the “cables” that connected the organs, making it look like it could also be the work of organ traffickers. After Will discovered a victim of an organ harvester , he talked to Hannibal about it. Then Hannibal made Will believe that not only this one, but also most of the so-called “Ripple victims” were in fact the work of organ traffickers that were discussing themselves as the Ripper to avoid detection all along. Making the FBI question the existence of the Ripper itself. This would explain why they couldn’t catch the Ripple even after all these years. He then used this chance to kill even more people (with the FBI believing that this was indeed the work of harvesters), misdirecting everyone. The similarities between the murders were so subtle that even Will was struggling to see the Ripper.
(Anticipation, Creative Thinking, Deception: Misdirection, Indirect Manipulation)

S1 Ep 8

-The episode starts, and we can see Will’s mental condition getting worse. Will is now hearing noises outside his house but when he gets out to check the noise he finds nothing. Afterwards he calls Bloom to his house in order to help him find whatever was producing the sound. After they leave the house to search, Will asks Anala if he thought this was a dait, with Alana humorously responding that the thought had never crossed her mind. Is at this moment that we start to see the chemistry between those two characters and the fact that they probably like each other.

-Later that day, Hannibal has an appointment with one of his patients. This particular patient has an obsession with Hannibal. He wants to be Hannibal so he starts psychoanalyzing people, starting with his friends. The patient states that one of his friends is acting very unusually lately, saying dark things and then passing them as a joke. This sudden behavior has pushed Hannibal’s patient to search for psychopathic behavior on the internet and conclude that his friend might be a psychopath. Hannibal says that “Psychopaths are not crazy, they are fully aware of what they do and what their consequences are”.
(Insight)

-The FBI finds a body at the . The body was a member of the orchestra, someone cut his throat and stuck the neck of a cello inside in order to play him like an instrument. When Will was analyzing the crime scene he suddenly spotted Hobbs in the background, watching him and clapping as he applauded him (another sign of his condition). (The killer was the friend of Hannibal’s patient, Tobias).

-In the FBI Headquarters, where they are analyzing the body, Will sits down beside his colleagues. After looking at the body again Will’s facial expression changes, and he said “I had to open you up to get a decent sound out of you”, which shocked everyone in the room. Making Will look like he was indeed slowly losing his mind. (This was mainly his condition speaking again).

-Afterwards, Will goes to Hannibal’s office to talk about the case. Hannibal displays extensive knowledge about music and the historical background of the music arts. He states that the first instruments were flutes made from human bones. Will sees the crime scene as a performance and Hannibal goes on to compare human beings with music, saying that we are both finite events and each of us is producing his own melodie. Some better than others, some not worth hearing again. Hannibal believes that this killer is a poet and a psychopath, and Will adds that he’s also a craftsman (because he was manipulating the vocal cords of the body to make them feel like Cleo strings). Hannibal asks if there was olive oil in the strings and Will confirms that there was. Hannibal then deduces that the killer was trying to create an original sound, based on the fact that olive oil hasn’t been used in the strings of instruments for over a century (the reason they used it was to expand the life of the strings and create a sweeter and more melodic sound). Will admits to Hannibal that he could hear what the killer was playing behind his eyes when he closed them. Upon perceiving frustration in Will, Hannibal asks him “What do you see behind closed eyes ?”. Will stops for a moment before answering “I see myself” (an easy lie for Hannibal to detect). Then Hannibal changes the subject back to the analysis of the crime. He asks Will about who the killer was performing for. Will isn’t sure about that, he thinks that he might be for another musician or even another killer. Hannibal then deduces that this performance is in fact a serenade, specifically made for a specific person to hear.
(Knowledge, Abstract Thinking, Deduction, Psychoanalysis, EU)

-Hannibal has another appointment with his patient (Tobias friend). He tells him that Tobias once again has started to say very dark things to him. He confesses to Hannibal that Tobias told him about his thoughts of opening a person up and playing him like a cello, and that some days after they found someone who was opened up to was played like a cello. Hannibal understood that the reason Tobias was saying all those things to his friend was because he knew that he was going to talk to him about it. The person Tobias was trying to impress by killing the member of the orchestra, was Hannibal. (EU, STP, Awareness)

-After Hannibal has taken Tobias’s invitation he decides to pay him a visit in his workshop (Tobias is a craftsman of musical instruments, mainly cellos). Hannibal carefully opened the door and stopped the little doorbell from ringing so that no one would hear him entering the workshop. Tobias was playing the violin when Hannibal entered and didn’t hear him coming (Hannibal didn’t want him to stop playing). When he saw Hannibal the two greeted each other. After talking for a while Hannibal reveals to Tobias his true intentions after Tobias said that it was terrible what happened to the member of the orchestra (referring to the murder victim) by saying “Not really, I can’t stop thinking that the orchestra will be better without him” and that “it seems like we are both comfortable playing between conversational notes”. After their talk, the situation was clear.
(Information Control, Methods)

-Will is in his house when he suddenly starts hearing weird sounds again. This time it sounds like there was a raccoon stuck in his chimney, so Will decided to open a hole to free it, but nothing was there. The next morning Alana passed by Will’s house to see how he was doing. Alana was always avoiding being alone in a room with Will because of her professional curiosity about Will’s condition. She also just wants people to live Will alone, because she cares about him. Will was slowly starting to lose control of his life those days because of his worsening condition, so in a desperate attempt to hold on to what was real he kissed Alana. She admitted to having feelings for Will but she knew that because of how she operates in a relationship this wouldn’t work.

-Hannibal invites Tobias to his house for dinner. At the dinner Tobias expresses to Hannibal his desire to kill his patient. Hannibal states that they are going to find him, but Tobias doesn’t care about that because he wants them to try, saying that “if anyone comes to search for me I will kill him”. Tobias also says that he was going to kill Hannibal too, until he followed him one night in a lonely road (the one Hannibal killed the doctor) and watched him do his job, stating that he did it “very well”. After that Hannibal says to Tobias that he was “reckless” because now he won’t be drawing attention just to himself. Tobias asks Hannibal to work with him and be his friend, saying that he could use someone who can understand him and see the world as he does. Hannibal says that “I know exactly how you feel” but he doesn’t want to be his friend because Hannibal views him as inferior to him (as food). In fact Hannibal was planning to kill Tobias when he invited him to his house for dinner, but suddenly the doorbell rings. It was Will. Throughout this entire conversation Hannibal never lost his composure even for a second. Hannibal goes to the door and lets him in, but when they go to the table Tobias has left via a glass door. Will sees the dinner table and asks if Hannibal had a guest, with him replying that it was a colleague and that he had to leave because of an urgent call. He continues by saying that “This benefits you, because I have dessert for two” (changing the subject and avoiding the situation). Hannibal goes on to ask Will about his kiss with Alana, asking about her reaction to it. Will says that she believes that this couldn’t work and Hannibal agrees, pointing out that Will would resent her if she ever tried to study him, which she would feel an obligation to do. Will knows all this which pushes Hannibal to ask him why he kissed her in the first place. Will avoids the subject but Hannibal knows that there was a deeper reason Will felt compelled to do such a thing. Deducing that because of his recent experience (sleepwalking, paranoia) Will feels that he is losing control of reality. Hannibal is able to understand Will's deteriorated mental state as it progresses. He goes on to say that kissing Alana was just a desperate attempt to hold on to what seemed to be the only real thing at the time. Hannibal then decides to help Will catch the killer, by telling him that a patient of his told him today that he suspects one of his friends might be involved with the murder at the orchestra and that he wasn’t sure if he needed to say something because of his doctor-patient confidentiality. He then gives Will enough details to find him.
(EM, Composure, Adaptability, Psychological Analysis, EU, STP, Information Control, Decision Making)

-The next day, Will goes to Tobias's house to question him, but while he's questioning him he hears an accident outside, which sends him out. In truth, it was his imagination, but while he was outside, Tobias took the opportunity to kill two policemen and leave.

-Meanwhile Hannibal is having another appointment with his patient when sadly Tobias arrives into Hannibal’s office, stating that he has killed two people. His friend tries to persuade him into giving himself up to the police when Hannibal decides to kill his patient (Tobias friend) by breaking his neck with a single move, effortlessly putting him on the ground. The reason Hannibal did that was to take away Tobias' wish to kill him. After that Tobias attacks Hannibal and the two fight instead at Hannibal's office. The battle is excruciating but at the end Hannibal is able to beat Tobias by using the ladder in his office to trap and break Tobias’s hand. After that Hannibal is able to effortlessly take down Tobis with a single punch to the throat. With Tobias incapacitated, Hannibal uses a heavy object in his office to break Tobias’s skull, killing him, while also covering his hands with a pocket square so that he would leave any fingerprints on the weapon. Hannibal was able to anticipate Tobias arrival from 3 things: 1) he knew that if anyone was trying to search Tobias for the murder in the orchestra, Tobias would kill them (as he stated himself in Hannibal’s dinner table). So Hannibal was sending the police after him knowing that they were going to die. That’s also why he sent Will after him as well, so that he could further drive and test his psychological state. 2) he knew that if he survived the police investigation he  would suspect that someone had sent them to him, the only 2 people with a motive to send the police would be either Tobias’s friend (aka Hannibal’s patient) or Hannibal himself. So the logical conclusion would be that if he survives the police he would then immediately arrive at Hannibal’s office. Later on, Jack with the FBI arrived at Hannibal’s office, where they are investigating the crime scene. Hannibal is using his acting skills to appear more vulnerable and fragile. Jack is confused so he asks Hannibal some questions, like why would the killer immediately come to your office after he just killed 2 police officers?. Hannibal tells Jack that he had come to kill his patient and that his patient was telling Hannibal less than he knew. This story made sense considering that Hannibal had informed Will about the connection between his patient and the supposed killer before the incident itself. So Hannibal appears to be the victim in this situation. A good psychiatrist who found himself in the middle of a fight. For that reason Hannibal was cleared from any suspicion.
Hannibal also indirectly accuse Will at this moment, since the last guy tobias saw before coming here was will graham, it literally make it looks like Will asked him to kill Hannibal

Episode Summary - Hannibal met someone who could understand him, but he didn’t want to be his friend. So Hannibal created a plan to kill both him and his patient, while also covering his tracks. Hannibal knew that Tobias would attack the police if they ever found him or suspect him, so he deliberately sent the police to his house via Will in order to draw him out. After that, Hannibal called his patient to his office for an appointment anticipating that Tobias would come to his house after he escaped from the police. So Hannibal was then able to kill both Tobias and his patient at the same time while also remaining as the victim in this situation (gaining further sympathy from Jack and the FBI) while also testing Will’s psychological state by sending him after Tobias (further pushing him psychologically).
(Anticipation, STP, Planning, Indirect Manipulation, Setting Traps, Risks Assessment, Fabrication, Deception: Acting Skills, Creativity Thinking, Battle IQ)

S1 Ep 9

-The episode starts with another crime scene, this time on a beach. Jack asks Hannibal to analyze the scene and Will gets to work. Will uses his ability to get into the mind of this specific killer in order to understand him and profile him. But when the analysis of the crime scene was completed, Will suddenly opened his eyes and found himself in Hannibal’s office, without knowing how he got there. Hannibal then opened the door and let Will in. Will said that he had no idea how he got here, it was like he blinked and then appeared in Hannibal’s office, but this time he wasn’t sleeping. Hannibal said that his car was outside which means that Will drove 3 hours without remembering anything. He lost time. Will is clearly frustrated and thinks that somethings really wrong with him. Hannibal says that Will has been dissociating, and that it’s a defense mechanism for consistent abuse. Will says that he is not abused.
Hannibal explains to Will that the feelings he’s recreating when he uses his personality disorder are overwhelming him, so when Will decided to stay in the field with Jack he created an emotional abuse. Will says that it feels good to save lives but Hannibal answers by saying that “I don’t care about other lives Will, I care about your life” (emotional manipulation). Will talks about his symptoms (sleepwalking, hallucinations and now loss of time) and thinks that he might need a brain scan. Hannibal deflects this theory by saying that Will is looking for something to escape the truth (misdirecting Will’s  accurate theory that the problem is in fact neurological and not mental illness). Hannibal explains that Will was on the beach when the association happened. He goes on to say that Will looked at the crime scene, his brain couldn’t handle the emotional distress so he disassociated, and lost time. Hannibal tries to gaslight Will one again by saying that he empathize with all of those killers Jack has sent him that he loses his mind in them, and asks Will about what would happen if the next time he would lost time hurt himself or someone else (indirectly creating the idea in Will’s mind of his larger feat from the beginning of the series, which is to become the people he’s hunting). Hannibal has successfully managed to make Will think that all of those symptoms are the cause of trauma from all the previous cases and Will personally disorder abuse instead of a neurological problem.
(Psychological/Emotional Manipulation, Gaslighting, EM, Verbal Deception)

-The next day Will goes to Jack's office to apologize for his behavior yesterday but Jack states that Will was fine and didn’t do anything weird (which means that even when he loses time, Will is still functional and acts normal, which makes it look more like Will suppressed his memory’s because of trauma). Jack gets worried with Will’s sudden questions and asks Will if everything is alright. Will admits to Jack that all of those cases have got to him and he thought it was more obvious but it seems like it was acting fine. Jack asks him about his state today and Will says that he’s fine.

-Will arrives at the FBI Headquarters Lab, where they have moved all the bodies from the crime scene. This time he’s much more scared to face them because he thinks that this is what causes his symptoms to appear. Nevertheless, he does his best to try and profile the killer.

-After that we can see Will in his classroom, giving a lecture about the case they are currently working on in front of some students, when Alana suddenly interrupts him. We can then see that there was no one else in the classroom except Will himself. Which means that his hallucinations had gone much worse.

-Nicholas Boyle’s body is discovered. So Jack has called Hannibal, Will and Alana to his office to analyze the situation. Jack suspects that Abigail Hobbs was in some way involved with her father’s crimes and maybe even the death of her friend (episode 3 throwback). So he wants Abigail to look at the body and testify. Alana disagrees with Jack, believing that Abigail is not in the right state of mind to do such a thing, but Hannibal states that Jack doesn’t seem like he is willing to hear other options right now. At the end Jack decides to bring Abigail to see the body and Will thinks that this could go really bad, but Hannibal says that “perhaps she’s stronger than we think”. Knowing that she will not break from this.
(EU, Anticipation, Psychoanalysis)

-Abigail sees the body in front of Jack and Dr Bloom, but as Hannibal expected, she doesn’t break despite Jack pressuring questions. So After Abigail testifies that she had no connection with the body Jack lets her go.

-After the interview, Hannibal arrives at Abigail’s hospital room. He says that it’s hard seeing a monster, but Abigail replies by saying that Nicholas was not a monster, in which Hannibal asks “are you?” (Guilt Tripping). Hannibal is not happy with Abigail, uncovering the body they hide, but Abigail thinks that after the FBI had asked them those questions they wouldn’t search the situation further. Hannibal points out that with what Abigail did, she managed to get Jack’s attention. He goes on to say that she had betrayed his trust and put his life on the line, by also committing “I deserve more than that” (trying to make Abigail feel bad about what he did). Hannibal also asks what would happen if he can’t trust her, creating the feeling in Abigail to win his approval again. (Psychological/Emotional Manipulation: Guilt Tripping)

-The next day, Will visits Nicholas' body in the FBI Headquarters and by using his ability he’s able to connect that Abigail is the one who murdered him.

-Will immediately arrives at Hannibal’s office to confront him about what he just learned.
